> 162	## 3. Data Flow Diagrams
> 163	
> 164	### 3.1 Render a faceless video (FR-VS-014/009/019/026 · happy path)
> 165	
> 166	```
> 167	Léo (UI)          useFacelessRender   video-faceless-render        n8n+vision_mcp+chroma        Supabase
> 168	 │                    │                      │                          │                          │
> 169	 │──[Gerar vídeo]────▶│                      │                          │                          │
> 170	 │  {product_id}      │──POST JWT───────────▶│                          │                          │
> 171	 │                    │                      │──auth.getUser()──────────────────────────────────────▶│ 401 se inválido
> 172	 │                    │                      │──resolve BYOK (Gemini)──────────────────────────────▶│ decrypted_user_api_keys
> 173	 │                    │                      │   402 <service>_not_configured se ausente            │
> 174	 │                    │                      │──deduct_mco_coins(uid, VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER)───────▶│ RPC · 402 saldo<custo
> 175	 │                    │                      │──INSERT video_renders(state=queued)─────────────────▶│
> 176	 │                    │                      │──trigger n8n e7e3beeb (Bearer VIDEO_STUDIO_PAT)──────▶│ (via bridge R5)
> 177	 │◀──202 {render_id, charged_mco}───────────│                          │                          │
> 178	 │                    │                      │   …worker async (sem teto Edge)…                     │
> 179	 │                    │                      │   script(Gemini)→image(Pollinations)→voice(VoxCPM)   │
> 180	 │                    │                      │   →overlays(Remotion:8088)→[frame inicial+final]     │
> 181	 │                    │                      │──QA GATE: vision.compare(frames) via PAT────────────▶│ vision_mcp (BYOK, 0 mco)
> 182	 │                    │                      │   inconsistente → state=qa_failed (NÃO gasta i2v)    │
> 183	 │                    │                      │   consistente → LTX i2v (Ken-Burns fallback)→FFmpeg  │
> 184	 │                    │                      │──upload MP4(≥100KB)─────────────────────────────────▶│ bucket video-studio-assets
> 185	 │                    │                      │──vision.analyze_video QA + mesh.consolidate_reference▶│ vision_mcp → mcorch_nodes (embed)
> 186	 │                    │                      │──upsert roteiro/verdict─────────────────────────────▶│ mcorch_chroma (API v2)
> 187	 │                    │                      │──UPDATE video_renders(state=done, storage_key)──────▶│
> 188	 │                    │                      │   (falha pós-débito → erase_video_artifacts? não;    │
> 189	 │                    │                      │    refund idempotente por render_id + infra_health_logs)
> 190	 │──[poll]───────────▶│──video-render-poll──▶│──SELECT (tenant-bound; outro tenant→404)────────────▶│
> 191	 │◀──{state:done, video_url(signed), node_id}│                          │                          │
> 192	```
> 193	
> 194	### 3.2 Open the HyperFrames editor + deterministic render (FR-VS-021/024/025)
> 195	
> 196	```
> 197	Marina (UI)        Route/App.tsx         HyperFramesEditor     video-hyperframes-render    hyperframes-render (ephemeral)
> 198	 │                    │                       │                       │                          │
> 199	 │──/dashboard/canvas/video──────────────────▶│ (rota ANTES de canvas/:id — FR-VS-022)           │
> 200	 │  (ProtectedRoute → /auth se sem sessão)    │ embed nativo @hyperframes/studio                 │
> 201	 │──[edita HTML/props na timeline + preview]──▶│ (estado interno do pacote; hot reload)           │
> 202	 │──[Render]──────────────────────────────────▶│──POST JWT {html,props,media_refs}──────────────▶│
> 203	 │                    │                       │                       │──auth.getUser()──────────────────────────▶ 401
> 204	 │                    │                       │                       │──deduct_mco_coins(uid, VIDEO_HYPERFRAMES_RENDER)─▶ 402
> 205	 │                    │                       │                       │──INSERT video_renders(queued)             │
> 206	 │                    │                       │                       │──spawn EPHEMERAL container────────────────▶│ (no creds em env · rede restrita)
> 207	 │                    │                       │                       │                          │ headless Chrome render → frames
> 208	 │                    │                       │                       │                          │ FFmpeg → MP4 determinístico
> 209	 │                    │                       │                       │◀─upload MP4──────────────│──▶ bucket video-studio-assets
> 210	 │◀──202 {render_id}──────────────────────────│◀──poll {state:done, video_url}──────────────────│
> 211	 │   (2 renders do mesmo input → sha256 igual · NFR-VS-016)                                       │
> 212	```
> 213	
> 214	### 3.3 Per-user BYOK resolution (R1 · FR-VS-005/006 · fail-closed)
> 215	
> 216	```
> 217	edge fn / container leg                          Supabase (VIEW Vault)
> 218	 │── needs Gemini key ──────────────────────────▶│
> 219	 │   SELECT gemini_api_key                        │ decrypted_user_api_keys
> 220	 │   FROM decrypted_user_api_keys                 │  WHERE user_id = <sub verificado>
> 221	 │◀── key (decrypted, server-side only) ──────────│
> 222	 │   key presente → usa a chave do tenant (BYOK; QA custo 0)
> 223	 │   key ausente  → HTTP 402 { error:'gemini_not_configured',
> 224	 │                            action:'Configure suas credenciais em /dashboard/settings' }
> 225	 │   NUNCA Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY') em path user-facing (FR-VS-006/NFR-VS-010)
> 226	 │   env global SÓ em cron/service-role/onboarding default (sem auth.uid())
> 227	```
> 228	
> 229	### 3.4 mcoCoins deduct + refund-on-failure (R2 · FR-VS-009/010/011)
> 230	
> 231	```
> 232	edge fn entrypoint                               Supabase
> 233	 │── (após JWT + BYOK resolve) ──────────────────▶│
> 234	 │   POST /rest/v1/rpc/deduct_mco_coins           │  { p_user_id:<sub>, p_amount:<COIN_COST>,
> 235	 │   (auth: SB_SECRET_KEY, server-side)           │    p_action:'video.faceless'|'video.hyperframes' }
> 236	 │◀── ok | 402 insufficient_balance ──────────────│  (atômico; saldo<custo → 402, nada debitado, 0 legs)
> 237	 │   …render…                                      │
> 238	 │   FALHA pós-débito:                             │
> 239	 │   refund idempotente (guard por render_id) ────▶│  UPDATE profiles.mco_balance += charged - already_refunded
> 240	 │   (espelha finalize_vision_job/refundMco;       │  + UPDATE video_renders.refunded_mco
> 241	 │    nunca lança; floor a favor do tenant)        │  + INSERT infra_health_logs(service='video-studio')
> 242	 │   delta mco_balance por SELECT prova o crédito (Lei 1)
> 243	```
> 244	
> 245	### 3.5 Mesh observation + semantic memory (R4 · FR-VS-026/027/028)
> 246	
> 247	```
> 248	Render success
> 249	  → INSERT mcorch_nodes { node_type:'observation', user_id (NUNCA NULL p/ tenant), project_id,
> 250	      metadata:{ fr_id:'FR-VS-026', render_id, engine, cost_mco, qa_confidence, product_id?,
> 251	                 provenance:{ origin:'tenant' } }, stability_score: 1.0 }
> 252	  → trigger/invoke embed-mcorch-node → pgvector(768) (grátis)
> 253	  → INSERT mcorch_edges (nó observation → nó do render/produto — instrumenta KPI)
> 254	  → upsert ChromaDB (API v2): { id: render_id, document: roteiro+verdict, metadata: tenant-scoped }
> 255	  → INSERT video_assets manifest (p/ erasure cascade FR-VS-031)
> 256	Render failure (qualquer leg)
> 257	  → INSERT infra_health_logs { service:'video-studio', event:'<leg>_failed', metadata:<allowlist> }
> 258	```
> 259	
> 260	---
> 261	
> 262	## 4. API Contract Definitions
> 263	
> 264	> Todo request passa, nesta ordem: **JWT verify → BYOK resolve (R1) → deduct_mco_coins (R2) → enqueue/spawn**. 401, 402 e 409 nunca debitam. Refund idempotente em falha pós-débito.
> 265	
> 266	### 4.1 `POST /functions/v1/video-faceless-render` (FR-VS-009/014)
> 267	
> 268	```typescript
> 269	// Request
> 270	interface FacelessRenderRequest {
> 271	  product_id?: string          // product-aware; opcional
> 272	  topic?: string               // tema livre
> 273	  project_id?: string          // tenant project scope (mesh isolation)
> 274	  scenes?: number              // cap por plano
> 275	  voice_consent?: boolean      // exigido se clonagem de voz identificável (FR-VS-032)
> 276	  delivery?: { telegram?: boolean }   // entrega opcional; link soberano sempre
> 277	}
> 278	// Response (HTTP 202)
> 279	interface FacelessRenderResponse {
> 280	  status: 'queued'
> 281	  render_id: string
> 282	  charged_mco: number          // VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER
> 283	  estimated_legs: number
> 284	}
> 285	// mcoCoins deduction point:
> 286	// → index.ts ~L50: APÓS auth.getUser() + resolve BYOK Gemini (402 se ausente),
> 287	//   ANTES de enfileirar / disparar n8n:
> 288	//   await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', {
> 289	//     p_user_id: user.id, p_amount: COIN_COSTS.VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER,
> 290	//     p_action: 'video.faceless', p_context: { render_id } })
> 291	//   → HTTP 402 'insufficient_balance' se saldo < custo (nada debitado, 0 legs)
> 292	// → refund idempotente (por render_id) em QUALQUER falha de leg pós-débito (FR-VS-011)
> 293	```
> 294	
> 295	### 4.2 `POST /functions/v1/video-hyperframes-render` (FR-VS-010/024/025)
> 296	
> 297	```typescript
> 298	interface HyperframesRenderRequest {
> 299	  template_id?: string         // template versionado; OU
> 300	  html?: string                // HTML/CSS/JS inline (UNTRUSTED → sandbox per-render OTD-VS-006)
> 301	  props?: Record<string, unknown>
> 302	  media_refs?: string[]        // assets do bucket do tenant
> 303	  fps?: number; width?: number; height?: number; duration_s?: number
> 304	}
> 305	interface HyperframesRenderResponse { status: 'queued'; render_id: string; charged_mco: number }
> 306	// deduction point: ~L45 — deduct_mco_coins(user.id, COIN_COSTS.VIDEO_HYPERFRAMES_RENDER, 'video.hyperframes').
> 307	// Render roda em CONTAINER EFÊMERO ISOLADO: zero credencial de tenant no env de render,
> 308	// rede restrita (HTML do tenant não alcança rede interna nem env). Falha → refund FR-VS-011.
> 309	```
> 310	
> 311	### 4.3 `POST /functions/v1/video-render-poll` (FR-VS-026 · read-only · tenant-bound)
> 312	
> 313	```typescript
> 314	interface RenderPollRequest { render_id: string }
> 315	interface RenderPollResponse {
> 316	  render_id: string
> 317	  state: 'queued' | 'running' | 'qa_gate' | 'done' | 'qa_failed' | 'failed'
> 318	  legs?: Array<{ name: string; status: string }>
> 319	  qa?: { consistent: boolean; confidence?: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low' }
> 320	  video_url?: string           // signed URL (sovereign delivery) — presente em 'done'
> 321	  refunded_mco?: number        // presente em 'failed'
> 322	  node_id?: string
> 323	}
> 324	// 0 mco. render_id de outro tenant → 404 (RLS-scoped). Sem leg de provider.
> 325	```
> 326	
> 327	### 4.4 Cost classes (4×-floor · espelhamento triplo · OTD-VS-007)
> 328	
> 329	| Operation | mco (provisório) | Modelo | Fix |
> 330	|-----------|------------------|--------|-----|
> 331	| `VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER` | ~125 (paridade canvas video DoP $0.56→4.0×) | `ceil(usd/$0.0045)` | medir 1 render real no 1º run pago (OTD-VS-007) |
> 332	| `VIDEO_HYPERFRAMES_RENDER` | ~12 (render local barato; análogo IMAGE_GENERATION 9–12) | idem | medir custo de compute do sandbox |
> 333	| `VIDEO_QA_VLM` | 0 (BYOK) / 2 (plataforma) | Gemini Flash ~$0.0004 | já calibrado (espelha vision-mcp) |
> 334	| `MESH_CONSOLIDATE_REFERENCE` | 1 | mesh write | já calibrado |
> 335	
> 336	Espelhamento triplo (G3 do `mcoin-cost-calibration.md`): `src/lib/billing.ts` `COIN_COSTS` + constante hardcoded na edge fn (Deno não importa `src/lib`) + asserção em `src/test/billing.test.ts`.
> 337	
> 338	---
> 339	
> 340	## 5. Security Design
> 341	
> 342	### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)
> 343	
> 344	| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |
> 345	|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|
> 346	| `hyperframes-render` (Puppeteer) | HTML/JS de tenant executa código arbitrário → RCE / SSRF / leitura de env / pivô de rede | Elevation of Privilege / Information Disclosure | **Critical** | **Sandbox per-render** (container efêmero, `cap_drop`, `no-new-privileges`, rede restrita egress-only, ZERO credencial de tenant no env); `/security-review` obrigatório; bloquear render multi-tenant até selado (OTD-VS-006 · FR-VS-025) |
> 347	| `open-design` daemon (compartilhado) | BYOK de um tenant resolvido do config GLOBAL `/app/.od/media-config.json` → quota/receita/risco vazam cross-tenant (fraude por design) | Information Disclosure / Tampering | **Critical** | Re-arquitetura per-user (OTD-VS-002): daemon per-job / `od` stateless / container por tenant; manter single-tenant EXPLÍCITO até selar; gate de GA (FR-VS-007) |
> 348	| `design.mcorch.com` (CF→origin) | Superfície exposta com `OD_API_TOKEN=development_token` + origins abertos → acesso não-autenticado ao editor | Spoofing | High | `OD_API_TOKEN` forte rotacionado + `OD_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://login.mcorch.com` + CF WAF Skip rule (libera não-browser legítimo sem abrir geral); confirmar via origin `--resolve` (curl cru retorna challenge) (FR-VS-003 · NFR-VS-012) |
> 349	| `video-faceless-render` / `-hyperframes-render` | Acesso não-autenticado dispara render caro | Spoofing | High | JWT validation no entrypoint (§5.3) antes de qualquer lógica (FR-VS-001/002) |
> 350	| mcoCoins deduction | Bypass client-side / mint / drain | Tampering | **Critical** | `deduct_mco_coins` RPC server-side ONLY; nunca client-side; refund crédito-positivo idempotente (R2 · FR-VS-009/011) |
> 351	| `video_renders` / `video_assets` | Leitura cross-tenant de renders/assets | Information Disclosure | **Critical** | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` default-deny; poll tenant-bound (404 p/ render alheio) |
> 352	| BYOK resolution path | Chave global usada em path user-facing → atribuição/risco errados | Information Disclosure | High | Fail-closed per-user (R1); 402 `<service>_not_configured`; grep env global = 0 (NFR-VS-010) |
> 353	| Studio→vision_mcp (bridge R5) | PAT do studio vazado → uso indevido do vision_mcp | Spoofing / Elevation | Medium | PAT com scopes mínimos (`vision:read`/`mesh:write`; NUNCA `credentials:*`/`billing:*`); Origin gate do vision_mcp; rotação |
> 354	| VoxCPM (voz clonada) | Clonagem de voz de terceiro sem consentimento → dado biométrico (LGPD Art. 11) | Information Disclosure / Compliance | High | Consent gate (FR-VS-032): `voice_consent` obrigatório p/ amostra identificável; recusa não cobra; nunca treinar/expor template sem consentimento |
> 355	| Telegram delivery | Token de bot ausente/compartilhado vaza vídeos cross-tenant | Information Disclosure | Medium | Entrega soberana (signed URL) é o default; Telegram opcional per-user (OTD-VS-003); token nunca compartilhado |
> 356	| `infra_health_logs` (global-read) | PII per-tenant vaza na tabela de leitura global | Information Disclosure | Medium | Telemetry allowlist no chokepoint (sem PII per-tenant) — precedente vision-mcp |
> 357	
> 358	### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions
> 359	
> 360	```sql
> 361	-- video_renders (default-deny + tenant isolation)
> 362	ALTER TABLE video_renders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 363	
> 364	CREATE POLICY "video_renders_select_own"
> 365	  ON video_renders FOR SELECT
> 366	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 367	
> 368	CREATE POLICY "video_renders_insert_own"
> 369	  ON video_renders FOR INSERT
> 370	  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());
> 371	
> 372	-- UPDATE de estado/refund é server-side (service-role) — sem policy de UPDATE p/ authenticated.
> 373	-- RESTRICTIVE no-delete (financeiro/auditável): clientes não apagam render (refund/ledger).
> 374	CREATE POLICY "video_renders_no_client_delete"
> 375	  ON video_renders AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE
> 376	  USING (false);
> 377	
> 378	-- video_templates (tenant-scoped CRUD)
> 379	ALTER TABLE video_templates ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 380	CREATE POLICY "video_templates_all_own"
> 381	  ON video_templates FOR ALL
> 382	  USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());
> 383	
> 384	-- video_assets (SELECT-own; writes server-side)
> 385	ALTER TABLE video_assets ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 386	CREATE POLICY "video_assets_select_own"
> 387	  ON video_assets FOR SELECT
> 388	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 389	```
> 390	
> 391	### 5.3 JWT Validation Points
> 392	
> 393	Toda edge function de vídeo segue este padrão (NFR-VS-008):
> 394	```typescript
> 395	const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')
> 396	if (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 397	const { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(
> 398	  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')
> 399	)
> 400	if (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 401	// só então: resolve BYOK (R1) → deduct_mco_coins (R2) → enqueue/spawn
> 402	```
> 403	O `mcorch_vision_mcp` (alvo da identidade do studio) valida **ES256 JWKS** ou **PAT Bearer** + **Origin gate** (precedente vision-mcp SDD §2.1); a identidade do studio é um PAT emitido (FR-VS-004). O `delete-account` valida JWT do próprio tenant antes do cascade.
> 404	
> 405	### 5.4 Secrets Inventory (POR NOME — nunca valores)
> 406	
> 407	| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |
> 408	|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|
> 409	| `SB_SECRET_KEY` | todas as edge fns (PostgREST/RPC server-side) | Supabase Vault | on suspected leak (legada `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` REVOGADA) |
> 410	| `gemini_api_key` (per-user) | director + vision QA (BYOK) | `decrypted_user_api_keys` (Vault, RLS-own) | controlada pelo tenant |
> 411	| `VIDEO_STUDIO_PAT` | identidade do studio → vision_mcp/chroma (bridge R5) | Supabase Vault / `mcp_access_tokens` | rotação periódica; scopes mínimos |
> 412	| `OD_API_TOKEN` | `design.mcorch.com` (open-design daemon) | container env | **rotacionar JÁ** (substituir `development_token` — FR-VS-003) |
> 413	| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` | entrega opcional (per-user OU sistema) | Vault (AUSENTE hoje — OTD-VS-003) | provisionar; ausência ≠ bloqueio |
> 414	| `affiliate_config.app_id` / `.affiliate_tag` (per-user) | link afiliado ML + atribuição | `affiliate_config` (RLS-own) | controlada pelo tenant |
> 415	
> 416	> ⚠️ NUNCA commitar valores de secret. Apenas NOMES de chave aparecem na documentação.
> 417	
> 418	### 5.5 LGPD Erasure Cascade (FR-VS-031 · gate de GA)
> 419	
> 420	`erase_video_artifacts(p_user_id)` (SECURITY DEFINER, service-role, `search_path=''`) — espelha `erase_vision_artifacts`/`erase_lead`:
> 421	1. `DELETE` rows `video_renders`/`video_templates`/`video_assets` do tenant.
> 422	2. Storage API remove os objetos do bucket `video-studio-assets` (OTD-VM-026 lição: storage via API, não SQL).
> 423	3. `DELETE` nós/edges/embeddings de roteiro/verdict (`mcorch_nodes`) do tenant + purge do ChromaDB.
> 424	4. Purga config BYOK de vídeo + (para open-design) `OD_DATA_DIR`/`OD_MEDIA_CONFIG_DIR` + a config global de provider que carrega a chave BYOK (precedente Canvas Design OTD-008).
> 425	5. Teste de zero-resíduo: `SELECT` count antes/depois = 0, ≤24h. Migration passa `/security-review`.
> 426	
> 427	### 5.6 Per-User Credential Resolution (R1 · invariante de design)
> 428	
> 429	Ordem obrigatória (API Tenancy Model): (1) per-user `decrypted_user_api_keys` por `auth.uid()`; (2) env global SÓ em cron/service-role/onboarding default sem `auth.uid()`; (3) hard-fail 402 `{error, action}` se nenhum resolve. Anti-pattern banido: `Deno.env.get('<SERVICE>_API_KEY')` em path user-facing sem fallback per-user (grep = 0 · NFR-VS-010).
> 430	
> 431	---
> 432	
> 433	## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints
> 434	
> 435	| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |
> 436	|----------------|-------|--------|
> 437	| Docker network bridge `n8n_default` ⇄ `mcorch-sovereign-net` | Studio alcança vision_mcp (`:3200`) + chroma (`:8001`) p/ QA + memória | **Bloqueador material do MVP** (OTD-VS-001); sem ele, QA/chroma são prosa |
> 438	| `remotion-render:8088` (network `n8n_default`, NOT host-published) | Leg de overlays (HUD/captions) | OTD-VS-004: host `:8088` tem TAMBÉM um node process stray — escolher o engine canônico |
> 439	| `mcorch_vision_mcp` (`127.0.0.1:3200`, PAT Bearer) | Portão de consistência (FR-VS-019) + mesh write | Identidade do studio = PAT emitido (FR-VS-004); BYOK-billed (QA custo 0) |
> 440	| `mcorch_chroma` (`127.0.0.1:8001`, API v2) | Memória semântica de roteiro/vídeo (FR-VS-028) | Acessível só via bridge R5 |
> 441	| `n8n-n8n-1` workflow `e7e3beeb58f7411a` | Cérebro faceless (ativar de `active:false` + instrumentar) | Helper assets copiados p/ `/home/node/.n8n/assets/`; SFX via `docker exec` lavfi (FR-VS-015) |
> 442	| `studio-dashboard` (`127.0.0.1:8095`) | Ops/observabilidade headless | Sobe via `docker compose up -d --build` (recria 2 containers ad-hoc — downtime breve) |
> 443	| `design.mcorch.com` (CF→origin `137.131.243.179`) | Editor open-design (Sovereign-gated) | WAF Skip rule + token forte + origins restritos (FR-VS-003); CF bot-challenge bloqueia não-browser (403) |
> 444	| `infra_health_logs` | Novo `service='video-studio'`; eventos por leg/falha | Watchdog telemetry (allowlist — sem PII per-tenant) |
> 445	| `embed-mcorch-node` (Edge Fn, reuso) | Embed-on-insert dos nós de observação | pgvector(768), grátis |
> 446	| Storage bucket `video-studio-assets` (private) | Vídeo/imagem/áudio materiais (entrega soberana) | Lei 1; signed URL curto; cascade erasure |
> 447

> 449	
> 450	## 7. Migration Stubs
> 451	
> 452	```sql
> 453	-- Migration: <ts>_video_studio_renders.sql
> 454	
> 455	CREATE TABLE video_renders (
> 456	  id            uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
> 457	  user_id       uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
> 458	  project_id    uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 459	  engine        text NOT NULL CHECK (engine IN ('faceless','hyperframes')),
> 460	  state         text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued'
> 461	                  CHECK (state IN ('queued','running','qa_gate','done','qa_failed','failed')),
> 462	  product_id    uuid,
> 463	  topic         text,
> 464	  legs          jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
> 465	  qa            jsonb,                              -- { consistent, confidence }
> 466	  charged_mco   integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (charged_mco >= 0),
> 467	  refunded_mco  integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (refunded_mco >= 0 AND refunded_mco <= charged_mco),
> 468	  refunded_at   timestamptz,                        -- idempotency guard (refund 1×/render)
> 469	  storage_key   text,                               -- objeto no bucket (vídeo ≥100KB em 'done')
> 470	  voice_consent boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
> 471	  node_id       uuid,                               -- observação na malha (FR-VS-026)
> 472	  expires_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() + interval '24 hours',  -- poll-able ≥24h
> 473	  created_at    timestamptz DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
> 474	  updated_at    timestamptz DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
> 475	);
> 476	
> 477	ALTER TABLE video_renders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 478	-- (RLS policies de §5.2: select_own · insert_own · RESTRICTIVE no_client_delete)
> 479	
> 480	-- video_templates (HyperFrames reuse) — RLS all_own (§5.2)
> 481	CREATE TABLE video_templates (
> 482	  id          uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
> 483	  user_id     uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
> 484	  project_id  uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 485	  name        text NOT NULL,
> 486	  html        text NOT NULL,
> 487	  props       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
> 488	  version     integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

> 490	  updated_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
> 491	);

> 504	);

> 35	### 1.2 Text ERD (relacionamentos)
> 36	
> 37	```
> 38	auth.users (tenant) ──1:N──▶ video_renders            (user_id, ON DELETE CASCADE; id = PK poll-able)
> 39	                   ──1:N──▶ video_templates           (user_id; HyperFrames reuse)
> 40	                   ──1:N──▶ video_assets              (user_id; manifest de erasure)
> 41	                   ──1:1──▶ user_api_keys (VIEW)       (BYOK gemini/<video_provider> — Vault)
> 42	                   ──1:N──▶ mcorch_nodes (vídeo)       (user_id NOT NULL p/ tenant — NUNCA system node)
> 43	                   ──1:N──▶ affiliate_links           (monetização; content_id liga ao asset)
> 44	                   ──1:1──▶ profiles (mco_balance)     (ledger via deduct_mco_coins/refund)
> 45	
> 46	video_renders   ──1:N──▶ video_scenes                (render_id, ON DELETE CASCADE; loop faceless)
> 47	                ──1:N──▶ video_assets                (render_id → manifest dos artefatos materiais)
> 48	                ──1:1──▶ mcorch_nodes (observation)  (node_id; observação na malha por success)
> 49	                ──1:N──▶ affiliate_links             (content_id NON-NULL p/ link de vídeo)
> 50	video_assets    ──N:M──▶ mcorch_nodes                (node_id — alvo do cascade de erasure)
> 51	                ──1:N──▶ storage.objects             (storage_key no bucket video-studio-assets)
> 52	video_templates ──1:N──▶ video_renders              (template_id; reuso determinístico HyperFrames)
> 53	mcorch_nodes (observation) ──edge──▶ mcorch_nodes (produto/roteiro)  (instrumenta KPI de receita)
> 54	mcorch_nodes (roteiro/verdict) ──upsert──▶ mcorch_chroma            (memória semântica API v2)
> 55	```
> 56	
> 57	**Cardinalidade-chave:** um `video_render` é a raiz do ledger financeiro (charged/refunded atômico) e a raiz da cascata de artefatos. Um `video_asset` agrega o objeto de Storage + os `node_ids` derivados — apagar o tenant (ou chamar `erase_video_artifacts`) derruba renders → scenes → assets → objetos de Storage → nós/embeddings de roteiro em cascata (FR-VS-031). O `video_template` é a única entidade Master per-tenant (reuso determinístico — argumento Enterprise PR-VS-022).

> 146	## 3. Supabase Physical Schema Stubs
> 147	
> 148	Os `CREATE TABLE`/RLS/índices/RPC físicos estão selados no **SDD §7 (Migration Stubs)** e **§5.2 (RLS Policies)** — autoridade única copy-ready para os arquivos de migration. Resumo das migrations (ordem de aplicação):
> 149	
> 150	| # | Migration file (stub) | Cria | RLS | Índices |
> 151	|---|----------------------|------|-----|---------|
> 152	| 1 | `<ts>_video_studio_renders.sql` | `video_renders` (+CHECK engine/state, `refunded_mco ≤ charged_mco`) | default-deny; SELECT/INSERT own; UPDATE service-role; **RESTRICTIVE no client delete** (ledger financeiro) | `(user_id, state)`, `(user_id, created_at DESC)`, `expires_at` |
> 153	| 2 | `<ts>_video_studio_scenes.sql` | `video_scenes` | default-deny; SELECT own; writes service-role | `(render_id, scene_index)` UNIQUE, `user_id` |
> 154	| 3 | `<ts>_video_studio_templates.sql` | `video_templates` | default-deny; ALL own (CRUD tenant-scoped) | `user_id`, `project_id` |
> 155	| 4 | `<ts>_video_studio_assets.sql` | `video_assets` | default-deny; SELECT own; writes service-role | `render_id`, `user_id`, `retention_until` |
> 156	| 5 | `<ts>_video_studio_byok.sql` | coluna BYOK condicional + re-create da família trigger/VIEW Vault | herdada (VIEW Vault mascarada) | — |
> 157	| 6 | `<ts>_video_studio_erase_rpc.sql` | `erase_video_artifacts(p_user_id)` SECURITY DEFINER `search_path=''` | EXECUTE revogado de PUBLIC/anon; GRANT authenticated (own via guard) + service_role (sweep) | — |
> 158	
> 159	**Padrão de cobrança:** nenhuma das tabelas tem coluna de embedding própria — roteiro/verdict embedam em `mcorch_nodes`/Chroma (§4). Tabelas financeiras (`video_renders`) seguem a família **RESTRICTIVE no-delete** de `creative_metrics`/`autopilot_cycles` (clientes nunca apagam o ledger). **Gate house:** cada migration passa `/security-review` independente ANTES do commit (FMEA-011 + NFR-VS-011). Bucket `video-studio-assets` privado (config + policy de Storage).
> 160	
> 161	### 3.1 RPC de erasure — `erase_video_artifacts(p_user_id)` (FR-VS-031 · espelha `erase_vision_artifacts`/`erase_lead`)
> 162	
> 163	```sql
> 164	-- Migration: <ts>_video_studio_erase_rpc.sql (FR-VS-031 — LGPD cascade · gate de GA).
> 165	-- Espelha erase_vision_artifacts (20260615200000) + erase_lead: SECURITY DEFINER + search_path='' +
> 166	-- guard de tenant em CADA cascade DELETE (um node_ids[] envenenado nunca vira primitiva cross-tenant).
> 167	-- Storage: NÃO deletado em SQL (storage.objects é do storage-admin → 42501); RETURN storage_keys p/ o
> 168	-- CALLER (container erase-broker / delete-account, service-role) remover via Storage API (lição OTD-VM-026).
> 169	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.erase_video_artifacts(p_user_id uuid)
> 170	RETURNS jsonb
> 171	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = ''
> 172	AS $$
> 173	DECLARE
> 174	  v_keys text[];
> 175	  v_node_ids uuid[];
> 176	  v_nodes int; v_renders int;
> 177	BEGIN
> 178	  -- Guard de tenant (ledger-RPC rule): só o próprio tenant OU service-role pode disparar o cascade.
> 179	  IF NOT (p_user_id = auth.uid() OR auth.role() = 'service_role') THEN
> 180	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden' USING errcode = '42501';
> 181	  END IF;
> 182	
> 183	  SELECT array_agg(storage_key), array_agg(node_id)
> 184	    INTO v_keys, v_node_ids
> 185	    FROM public.video_assets WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 186	
> 187	  -- Cada DELETE re-escopado por user_id (defense-in-depth — SECURITY DEFINER bypassa RLS).
> 188	  DELETE FROM public.mcorch_edges
> 189	   WHERE (source_id = ANY(COALESCE(v_node_ids,'{}')) OR target_id = ANY(COALESCE(v_node_ids,'{}')))
> 190	     AND user_id = p_user_id;
> 191	  DELETE FROM public.mcorch_nodes
> 192	   WHERE id = ANY(COALESCE(v_node_ids,'{}')) AND user_id = p_user_id;   -- leva o embedding junto
> 193	  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_nodes = ROW_COUNT;
> 194	  DELETE FROM public.video_assets    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 195	  DELETE FROM public.video_scenes    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 196	  DELETE FROM public.video_renders   WHERE user_id = p_user_id;          -- CASCADE leva scenes/assets remanescentes
> 197	  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_renders = ROW_COUNT;
> 198	  DELETE FROM public.video_templates WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 199	
> 200	  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
> 201	    'erased', true, 'renders', v_renders, 'nodes', v_nodes,
> 202	    'storage_keys', to_jsonb(COALESCE(v_keys,'{}'))   -- caller remove via Storage API + purga Chroma + OD_DATA_DIR
> 203	  );
> 204	END $$;
> 205	
> 206	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.erase_video_artifacts(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
> 207	GRANT  EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.erase_video_artifacts(uuid) TO authenticated, service_role;
> 208	```
> 209	
> 210	> O `delete-account` (edge fn, service-role) chama esta RPC e ADICIONALMENTE: remove os objetos de Storage (`storage_keys` retornados via Storage API), faz purge das coleções do ChromaDB do tenant (§4.5), e dispara a limpeza do `OD_DATA_DIR`/`OD_MEDIA_CONFIG_DIR` + config global de provider do open-design que carrega a chave BYOK (precedente Canvas Design OTD-008).
> 211	
> 212	---
> 213	
> 214	## 4. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes
> 215	
> 216	### 4.1 Node types introduzidos / tipados
> 217	
> 218	| node_type | Origem | Regime | stability_score inicial | revenue_impact |
> 219	|-----------|--------|--------|------------------------|----------------|
> 220	| `observation` | render `success` (FR-VS-026) — um nó por render | **decai** (FSRS-6) | 1.0 | 0 (enabler interno); recalculado quando o link afiliado do vídeo converte (FR-VS-017) |
> 221	| `video_script` | roteiro product-aware do director (FR-VS-028) — base de reuso/recomendação | **decai** | 0.6 | 0 (corpus); upsell quando vira venda |
> 222	| `video_qa_verdict` | verdict do olho criativo (FR-VS-020) persistido na malha (1 mco, opcional) | **decai** | 0.6 | 0 |
> 223	
> 224	> Conteúdo de tenant **NUNCA** é system node (`user_id IS NULL`) — guard server-side (precedente vision-mcp NFR-VM-009). `revenue_impact` do nó `observation` é atualizado a cada conversão atribuída do link afiliado ML ligado ao asset (`affiliate_links.content_id`).
> 225	
> 226	### 4.2 Edge types
> 227	
> 228	| relation_type | Source → Target | Significado |
> 229	|---------------|-----------------|-------------|
> 230	| (edge ao render) | nó `observation` → nó do produto/roteiro | instrumenta KPI de receita por render (R4) |
> 231	| `DERIVES_FROM` | nó `video_qa_verdict` → nó `observation` do render | lineage do verdict (padrão da casa) |
> 232	| `DERIVES_FROM` | nó `video_script` reusado → roteiro original | recomendação de próximo tema (PR-VS-021) |
> 233	
> 234	### 4.3 Embedding fields
> 235	
> 236	Conteúdo textual do nó (`content` + `title` = roteiro + verdict) → `embed-mcorch-node` (Edge Fn existente, **reuso** — sem encanamento novo): OpenRouter `text-embedding-3-small` → fallback Gemini `text-embedding-004`, **768 dims**, HNSW cosine. Embed-on-insert **grátis** via o trigger existente (pg_net async — nunca bloqueia o insert). Espelhamento Chroma (§4.5) para busca semântica adicional.
> 237	
> 238	### 4.4 Metadata shape de um nó de observação de render
> 239	
> 240	```json
> 241	{
> 242	  "fr_id": "FR-VS-026",
> 243	  "br_id": "BR-VS-008",
> 244	  "render_id": "<uuid>",
> 245	  "engine": "faceless | hyperframes",
> 246	  "cost_mco": 125,
> 247	  "qa_confidence": "high | medium | low",
> 248	  "product_id": "<external_id ML | null>",
> 249	  "scenes": 5,
> 250	  "provenance": { "origin": "tenant" },
> 251	  "retention_until": "<ISO; > now(); ≤ teto §7>"
> 252	}
> 253	```
> 254	
> 255	### 4.5 ChromaDB memória semântica (FR-VS-028 · via bridge R5)
> 256	
> 257	Roteiro + verdict de QA também são `upsert`ados no `mcorch_chroma` (API v2, `127.0.0.1:8001`) — id = `render_id`, document = roteiro+verdict, metadata tenant-scoped (`user_id`/`project_id`). Pesquisável por `mesh.search`/Chroma após o render (base de reuso/recomendação). **Bloqueador material:** o `upsert` Chroma SÓ resolve após a ponte de rede R5 (OTD-VS-001) — antes disso é prosa. A coleção do tenant é purgada no cascade de erasure (§3.1 caller).

> 261	## 5. Tenant Isolation Map
> 262	
> 263	| Table / Object | Scope | Policy resumo | System-node caveat |
> 264	|----------------|-------|---------------|--------------------|
> 265	| `video_renders` | `user_id = auth.uid()` + `project_id` | SELECT/INSERT own; UPDATE service-role; **RESTRICTIVE no client delete** | poll alheio → 404 (FR-VS-026) |
> 266	| `video_scenes` | `user_id = auth.uid()` | SELECT own; writes service-role | — |

> 271	| `mcorch_nodes` (vídeo) | `user_id + project_id` | conteúdo de tenant **NUNCA** `user_id IS NULL` | **proibido** system node p/ tenant — guard server-side; violação = bug crítico |
> 272	| `mcorch_edges` | mesmo par do nó | herda isolamento | — |

> 274	
> 275	> ⚠️ **Buraco de isolamento conhecido (OTD-VS-002 · BR-VS-RISK-003):** o open-design resolve provider do config GLOBAL `/app/.od/media-config.json` — o `od` é cliente fino de um daemon **compartilhado**, então `OD_MEDIA_CONFIG_DIR` por invocação NÃO isola. Hoje single-tenant EXPLÍCITO; multi-tenant exige re-arquitetura (daemon per-job / `od` stateless / container por tenant) ANTES do 2º tenant.

> 300	| voz clonada (VoxCPM) | amostra de pessoa identificável | consentimento explícito; nunca treinar/expor template sem | — | "Clonagem de voz não autorizada." | FR-VS-032 / LGPD Art. 11 |

> 304	## 7. Retention & Archival Policy (LGPD Art. 16 / 18 · gate de GA)
> 305	
> 306	| Classe de dado | Retenção | Mecanismo de expiração | Erasure sob demanda |
> 307	|----------------|----------|------------------------|---------------------|
> 308	| `video_renders` (ledger) | indefinida enquanto a conta existe (auditável); `expires_at` é só TTL de poll | poll TTL ≥24h; sem auto-delete do ledger | cascade via `delete-account` → `erase_video_artifacts` |
> 309	| `video_assets` + objetos (Storage `video-studio-assets`) | `retention_until` (tenant pode encurtar, nunca exceder o teto) | sweep diário in-container (`jobs/video-retention-sweep.ts`, espelha vision-mcp PROC-VM-008) | `erase_video_artifacts` + Storage API remove |
> 310	| `video_scenes` | acompanha o `video_render` | cascade do render | mesmo cascade |
> 311	| Nó de roteiro/observação/verdict + embeddings (`mcorch_nodes`) | acompanha o asset (`retention_until` no metadata) | sweep diário (mesmo cascade) | cascade `erase_video_artifacts` |
> 312	| Memória semântica (ChromaDB) | espelha o nó de roteiro | sweep + purge da coleção | purge no cascade (caller, §3.1) |
> 313	| Voz clonada (template biométrico, se persistido) | só com consentimento; deletada no cascade | — | cascade + revogação de consentimento (LGPD Art. 11) |
> 314	| Config BYOK de vídeo + open-design (`OD_DATA_DIR`/`OD_MEDIA_CONFIG_DIR` + config global de provider) | enquanto a conta existe | — | purgada no cascade (precedente Canvas Design OTD-008) |
> 315	| `infra_health_logs` (vídeo) | 90 dias (política global; allowlist sem PII per-tenant) | cron purge global | — |
> 316	
> 317	**Cascade de erasure (prova material NFR-VS-015 · gate de GA):** `SELECT count(*)` de renders/scenes/assets/nós/edges/embeddings/objetos de Storage **antes e depois** de `delete-account` = **zero resíduo em ≤24h**. Precedentes: `erase_vision_artifacts()` (vision-mcp) + `erase_lead()` (marketing-hub) + Canvas Design OTD-008. **Limite honesto:** a chave BYOK do open-design no config global é purgada explicitamente; cópias eventuais já transmitidas a providers externos dependem do DPA do provider (registro auditável, como em vision-mcp `provider_copies`).

> 58	## 3. FMEA Table
> 59	
> 60	> Severity (S), Occurrence (O), Detection (D): 1 (best) → 10 (worst). **RPN = S × O × D**. **Todo RPN > 100 exige ação recomendada (coluna obrigatória).** Cada FMEA mapeia um FR-VS crítico (FRD §2) e/ou risco de negócio (BRD §8). Os 4 OTD-VS estruturais (untrusted-HTML, shared-daemon, bridge, calibração) concentram o RPN mais alto.
> 61	
> 62	| ID | Function (FR/PROC) | Failure Mode | Effect | S | O | D | RPN | Recommended Action |

> 87	
> 88	## 4. Self-Optimization Loops
> 89	
> 90	> Loops fecham a malha autonômica: KPI fora de alvo → resposta autônoma (mega-brain-daemon / cron) → escalação humana se persistir. Espelham o padrão do watchdog + `autopilot-analyze`.
> 91	
> 92	| Loop ID | Trigger Condition | KPI Source | Autonomous Response | Escalation (human) |
> 93	|---------|------------------|------------|--------------------|--------------------|
> 94	| SOL-VS-001 | KPI-VS-001/002 render success rate < warn por 2 janelas consecutivas | `video_renders` | `mega-brain-daemon`: health-check dos containers `n8n-n8n-1`/`remotion-render`; restart do container unhealthy; re-bootstrap de assets (FR-VS-015) | Alerta Sovereign se < critical (< 85% faceless) |
> 95	| SOL-VS-002 | KPI-VS-004 QA pass rate < 60% (critical) | `video_renders.qa` + vision_mcp | Auto-ajuste de threshold do gate G3 + injeção de feedback no director Gemini (espelha `orchestrate-step` feedback injection); marcar `qa_failed` mais cedo | Alerta se persistir 7 dias (modelo/prompt drift) |
> 96	| SOL-VS-003 | KPI-VS-003 cost-per-video desvio > 30% (sub-margem) | `video_renders` | Disparar rotina de re-calibração (medir 1 render real, OTD-VS-007); congelar a classe de custo até recalibrar | Alerta Sovereign (decisão de pricing — Owner) |
> 97	| SOL-VS-004 | KPI-VS-018 LTX fallback rate > 85% (LTX indisponível) | `infra_health_logs` `ltx_quota_fallback` | Forçar modo Ken-Burns global temporário + sinalizar "movimento reduzido" no poll/UI; throttle de novos jobs faceless | Alerta Sovereign (cota GPU / provider de vídeo pago) |
> 98	| SOL-VS-005 | KPI-VS-010 refund rate > 15% (critical) | `video_renders` | Análise automática do `<leg>_failed` dominante em `infra_health_logs`; auto-heal da leg falha (restart container / retry); pausar enqueue se a leg estiver morta | Alerta Sovereign + abrir OTD se a causa for arquitetural |
> 99	| SOL-VS-006 | KPI-VS-011 double-charge guard ≥ 1 (qualquer ocorrência) | `video_renders` + `profiles` | **HALT** de novos renders pagos; reconciliação automática do delta `mco_balance`; refund corretivo idempotente | Alerta Sovereign IMEDIATO (incidente financeiro — Lei 1) |
> 100	| SOL-VS-007 | KPI-VS-016 burn rate aproxima do teto do plano do tenant | `profiles` + `video_renders` | Notificação in-app (PT-BR) ao tenant (< 10% da quota); sugerir upgrade Starter→Pro→Enterprise | — (sem auto-ação financeira) |
> 101	| SOL-VS-008 | KPI-VS-009 BYOK-configured rate < 40% (critical) | `user_api_keys` + `profiles` | Nudge in-app de onboarding BYOK (QA grátis via BYOK); destacar `/dashboard/settings` | — (decisão do tenant) |
> 102

> 104	
> 105	## 5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 106	
> 107	### 5.1 Watchdog Telemetry Events
> 108	
> 109	Novos `event` types escritos em `infra_health_logs` por este módulo (sempre `service='video-studio'`; payload sob **allowlist** — sem PII per-tenant na tabela global-read, FMEA-VS-009):
> 110	
> 111	| Event Type | Trigger | Severity | Payload (allowlist) |
> 112	|------------|---------|----------|---------------------|
> 113	| `render_submitted` | Job aceito (JWT+BYOK+deduct+enqueue OK) | info | `{render_id, engine, charged_mco, estimated_legs}` |
> 114	| `<leg>_failed` (`script`/`image`/`voice`/`motion`/`overlay`/`composite`/`qa`) | Leg falha após retries (PROC-VS-001) | error | `{render_id, leg, retry_count, error_code}` |
> 115	| `render_failed` | Render terminal `failed` após refund | error | `{render_id, engine, failed_leg, refunded_mco}` |
> 116	| `qa_gate_blocked` | Gate G3 reprova consistência (FR-VS-019) | warn | `{render_id, qa_confidence, divergent_attrs_count}` |
> 117	| `ltx_quota_fallback` | LTX ZeroGPU cota/timeout → Ken-Burns (FR-VS-016) | warn | `{render_id, motion_mode:'ken_burns'}` |
> 118	| `refund_applied` | Refund idempotente crédito-positivo aplicado | info | `{render_id, refunded_mco, already_refunded}` |
> 119	| `double_charge_detected` | `refunded_mco > charged_mco` OU delta inconsistente | error | `{render_id, charged_mco, refunded_mco}` |
> 120	| `asset_stub_rejected` | Vídeo < 100KB / stub JSON rejeitado (FR-VS-030) | warn | `{render_id, bytes}` |
> 121	| `byok_missing` | Provider per-user ausente → 402 (FR-VS-005) | warn | `{user_id_hash, service}` |
> 122	| `studio_pat_handshake_failed` | Handshake MCP+PAT do studio falha (R5 · FR-VS-004) | error | `{component:'vision_mcp', http_status}` |
> 123	| `bridge_dns_unresolved` | DNS de container vision_mcp/chroma não resolve (R5) | error | `{target_container}` |
> 124	| `sandbox_spawn_failed` | Container per-render efêmero não sobe (FR-VS-025) | error | `{render_id}` |
> 125	| `voice_consent_blocked` | Clonagem sem consentimento recusada (FR-VS-032) | warn | `{render_id}` |
> 126	| `retention_sweep` | Sweep diário de retenção (PROC-VS-007) | info | `{swept_count, errors_count}` |
> 127	| `erasure_residue_detected` | `delete-account` deixou resíduo (FR-VS-031) | error | `{user_id_hash, residual_table, count}` |
> 128	| `recovery_ok` | Health-check passa após self-heal | info | `{component, action}` |
> 129	
> 130	### 5.2 Auto-Recovery Script Stubs
> 131	
> 132	```bash
> 133	#!/bin/bash
> 134	# scripts/self-heal-video-studio.sh
> 135	# Triggered by mega-brain-daemon when render_failed / <leg>_failed rate spikes
> 136	# (SOL-VS-001 / SOL-VS-005). Targets the Docker render mesh — NOT host nginx.
> 137	
> 138	set -euo pipefail
> 139	TS() { date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ; }
> 140	LOG_TABLE="infra_health_logs"
> 141	SERVICE="video-studio"
> 142	
> 143	echo "[$(TS)] Self-heal triggered for $SERVICE"
> 144	
> 145	# Step 1 — health of the render containers (engine canônico = container, OTD-VS-004)
> 146	for C in n8n-n8n-1 remotion-render mcorch_vision_mcp mcorch_chroma; do
> 147	  STATE=$(docker inspect -f '{{.State.Health.Status}}{{.State.Status}}' "$C" 2>/dev/null || echo "absent")
> 148	  echo "[$(TS)] $C -> $STATE"
> 149	  case "$STATE" in
> 150	    *unhealthy*|absent|*exited*)
> 151	      echo "[WARN] $C degraded ($STATE). Restarting..."
> 152	      docker restart "$C" || echo "[ERR] restart failed for $C"
> 153	      ;;
> 154	  esac
> 155	done
> 156	
> 157	# Step 2 — re-bootstrap n8n helper assets if missing (FR-VS-015 / FMEA-VS-013)
> 158	if ! docker exec n8n-n8n-1 test -f /home/node/.n8n/assets/ltx.js; then
> 159	  echo "[WARN] n8n render assets missing. Re-bootstrapping..."
> 160	  docker cp ./n8n/scripts/ltx.js        n8n-n8n-1:/home/node/.n8n/assets/ltx.js
> 161	  docker cp ./n8n/scripts/composite.sh  n8n-n8n-1:/home/node/.n8n/assets/composite.sh
> 162	  bash ./scripts/gen-sfx.sh   # docker exec ffmpeg lavfi SFX library
> 163	fi
> 164	
> 165	# Step 3 — verify the studio identity bridge (R5 · FR-VS-004 · FMEA-VS-003)
> 166	#   handshake MCP+PAT against vision_mcp via the bridged network (loopback-only origin gate preserved)
> 167	HS=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
> 168	  -H "Authorization: Bearer $VIDEO_STUDIO_PAT" \
> 169	  -H "Origin: https://login.mcorch.com" \
> 170	  http://mcorch_vision_mcp:3200/healthz || echo "000")
> 171	[ "$HS" = "200" ] || echo "[ERR] studio PAT handshake to vision_mcp failed (status=$HS)"
> 172	
> 173	# Step 4 — log recovery attempt (allowlist payload; via Supabase REST + SB_SECRET_KEY)
> 174	echo "[$(TS)] Self-heal pass complete for $SERVICE (handshake=$HS)"
> 175	# → INSERT infra_health_logs { service:'video-studio', event:'recovery_ok', metadata:{...allowlist} }
> 176	```
> 177	
> 178	> **Idempotência e blast radius:** o stub atua APENAS sobre containers da malha de render (Docker) — **nunca** toca `login.mcorch.com` (nginx/CloudPanel do host, fora do Docker). `docker restart` é idempotente; re-bootstrap de assets é no-op se já presentes. Refund nunca é acionado pelo self-heal (separação de responsabilidade financeira — só a edge fn refunda, por `render_id`).
> 179	
> 180	### 5.3 mega-brain-daemon Integration Points
> 181	
> 182	| Daemon Event | Condition | Action |
> 183	|-------------|-----------|--------|
> 184	| `health_check` | Cada 5 min (`scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh`) | Inspecionar `n8n-n8n-1`/`remotion-render`/`mcorch_vision_mcp`/`mcorch_chroma` + `video_renders` queue depth vs baseline; pulse ao Neural HUD via `get-infra-status` |
> 185	| `anomaly_detected` | `infra_health_logs` error rate (`<leg>_failed`/`render_failed`) > 5/hora (SOL-VS-005) | Executar `self-heal-video-studio.sh`; identificar a leg dominante; throttle de enqueue se a leg estiver morta |
> 186	| `quota_pressure` | `ltx_quota_fallback` > 85% por 1h (SOL-VS-004) | Forçar modo Ken-Burns global temporário; sinalizar Sovereign (cota GPU) |
> 187	| `financial_anomaly` | `double_charge_detected` ≥ 1 (SOL-VS-006) | **HALT** de renders pagos + reconciliação delta `mco_balance` + alerta Sovereign IMEDIATO |
> 188	| `bridge_down` | `bridge_dns_unresolved`/`studio_pat_handshake_failed` (R5) | Re-attach de rede / re-emitir/recarregar `VIDEO_STUDIO_PAT`; bloquear leg de QA até handshake OK |
> 189	| `recovery_confirmed` | Health-check passa após self-heal | INSERT `recovery_ok` em `infra_health_logs` + limpar flag de anomalia |
> 190

> 192	
> 193	## 6. SLA / SLO / SLI Definitions
> 194	
> 195	| Service | SLI (o que medimos) | SLO (nosso alvo) | SLA (consequência contratual) |
> 196	|---------|----------------------|------------------|-------------------------------|
> 197	| `video-faceless-render` submit API | p95 ACK latency (JWT+BYOK+deduct+enqueue) | ≤ 3s | Crédito de mcoCoins emitido se > 5s para > 1% das chamadas/mês |
> 198	| Faceless render pipeline (E2E) | p95 wall-clock (5 cenas, queued→done) | ≤ 12 min | Incident report + refund se > 20 min (SLA breach) e o render não concluir |
> 199	| `video-hyperframes-render` | p95 render wall-clock (clip ≤30s) | ≤ 90s | Refund se > 180s sem entrega |
> 200	| Vision QA gate (`compare` BYOK) | p95 latência do gate G3 | ≤ 6s | Não bloqueia entrega; degradação loga em `infra_health_logs` |
> 201	| mcoCoins deduct/refund | % renders com débito atômico correto + refund íntegro | 100% (double-charge = 0) | Reconciliação + crédito corretivo; incidente financeiro se ≥ 1 |
> 202	| Asset persistence | % renders `done` com objeto material ≥ 100KB no bucket | ≥ 99% | Auto-rejeição de stub + refund; nenhum cobrado por stub |
> 203	| Erasure cascade (LGPD) | % erasures com zero-resíduo ≤ 24h | 100% | Gate de GA bloqueado; remediação obrigatória antes do 2º tenant |
> 204	| Uptime das superfícies de vídeo (studio + editor + vision_mcp bridge) | % minutos disponível/mês (watchdog `*/5`) | ≥ 99.0% | Por contrato enterprise; incident report se < 99.0% |
> 205	| Knowledge Mesh embedding (observação de render) | % nós com embedding ≤ 5 min do insert | ≥ 95% | Auto-backfill via `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts` |

> 209	## 7. PDCA Improvement Cadence
> 210	
> 211	| Cycle | Phase | Activity | Owner | Frequency |
> 212	|-------|-------|----------|-------|-----------|
> 213	| video-studio Quality | **Plan** | Revisar FMEA-VS (RPNs) + alvos de KPI-VS; confirmar fechamento de OTD-VS-001..007 antes de cada Fatia | Sovereign | Quarterly (e ao abrir cada Fatia) |
> 214	| video-studio Quality | **Do** | Implementar a ação de RPN mais alto aberta (top: FMEA-VS-001 sandbox, FMEA-VS-002 BYOK open-design); medir 1 render real (OTD-VS-007) | Engineering | Per sprint |
> 215	| video-studio Quality | **Check** | Comparar KPI-VS vs alvo; recalibrar RPNs (sandbox/daemon selados ↓ Occurrence); recalibrar cost classes pós-medição | AIOS / `mega-brain-daemon` | Monthly |

## 🤖 Claude *(01:21:18)*

The input is valid. The issue must be with the tool itself. Let me provide my final assessment as plain text output since the StructuredOutput tool is not accepting valid JSON:

---

## SCALE-BATCH COVERAGE ASSESSMENT: VIDEO-STUDIO BoK

**DIMENSION:** Scale & batch production capacity for agency leverage (MCORCH B2B2C TAM multiplier)

**COVERAGE RATING:** **PARTIAL** (Single-render pipeline well-specified; batch/throughput/quota coverage DEFERRED to Future/Could Have)

---

### EVIDENCE OF COVERAGE (What IS documented)

1. **FRD §2 NFR-VS-005/006** — Single explicit scalability requirement: "Renders faceless concorrentes sem degradação (single-replica n8n/remotion)" with target "≥ 3 jobs em fila estável; LTX cota ~5 GPU-min/da respeitada via fallback"

2. **FRD §3 SLA definitions** — PROC-VS-001 submit ACK: p95 < 3s. Faceless E2E: ~5–12 min (p95). Single-job SLAs only; no batch throughput SLA.

3. **PRD §4 Could Have (PR-VS-020)** — "Render em lote (campanha de N vídeos por lista de produtos) com pré-débito atômico N × VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER + refund proporcional." Explicitly deferred to Future Scope. No schema or batch API defined.

4. **Process Flow §4** — "Viral Autopilot (FR-VA) — **consumidor futuro**" with description referencing campaign-run analogy but no queueing strategy materialized.

5. **SDD §1.2** — n8n workflow is single-replica ("ativar + instrumentar, não reescrever"). Zero horizontal scale architecture. docker-compose.yml recria 2 containers ad-hoc; no multi-instance orchestration.

6. **Data Model §1.1** — `video_renders` entity lacks batch coordination columns: NO `batch_id`, `batch_position`, `priority`, `queued_at`, `running_at`. No `batch_jobs` master entity.

7. **Quality Metrics §2 KPI Dashboard** — KPI-VS-001/002 are single-render success/time rates. Zero KPI for "videos/day per agency", "concurrent-job utilization", "queue depth per tenant", or "batch throughput (videos/hour)".

8. **FRD §4.1-4.2 API Contracts** — `POST /functions/v1/video-faceless-render` accepts 1 product_id + 1 topic per request; response is `{render_id, status: 'queued'}`. Zero `batch_items[]`, zero `batch_id`, zero batch state tracking.

---

### GAPS FOR AGENCY LEVERAGE

**Critical Missing Components:**

1. **No per-tenant quota/capacity model** — Agencies pay Starter/Pro/Enterprise for mcoCoins, but ZERO specification enforces "renders/day" or "concurrent renders" per plan tier. A single tenant can enqueue ∞ renders and saturate the 3-job ceiling indefinitely, starving other agencies.

2. **LTX ZeroGPU 5 GPU-min/day as undocumented HARD BOTTLENECK** — Agency targeting 10 faceless renders/day uses LTX i2v until ~10am, then hits free-tier ceiling and falls back to Ken-Burns (motion quality loss). Zero mitigation strategy: no paid LTX tier, no per-tenant GPU quota, no SOP for agencies. FMEA-VS-005 (RPN 120) only proposes telemetry.

3. **Zero batch/campaign queueing architecture** — No FIFO vs priority-queue decision. No per-tenant weighted-fair-queueing (WFQ) to prevent starvation. No Redis/RabbitMQ queue broker mentioned. No job scheduling strategy.

4. **No atomic pre-debit for batch with proportional refund** — Batch of 5 renders = 5 separate `deduct_mco_coins` RPC calls. Zero atomic "pre-debit 5 × VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER upfront, refund unprocessed N on partial failure" (PR-VS-020 says this is "análogo a campaign-run" but campaign-run itself is not materialized).

5. **Zero concurrent-render SLA beyond 3 jobs** — NFR-VS-005 targets "≥ 3 jobs stable". No SLA for 5/10/20 concurrent. No queue-wait SLA (p95 time from submit to running). No "max queue age before auto-refund" or "max queue depth before 429 Too Many Requests".

6. **No tenant isolation of shared compute** — n8n and remotion-render are single-instance per docker-compose. Tenant A monopolizes 3-job queue indefinitely → tenant B starved. Zero fair-queueing, zero resource reservation.

7. **Zero agency use-case journey for batch production** — PRD §2 has Léo (solo creator) and Marina (agência operadora), but Marina's journey is "edita HTML/props **per cliente**, renderiza MP4" — per-CLIENT, not per-DAY or per-BATCH. Zero scenario: "render 30 videos for 3 clients, 5 videos each, by EOD".

8. **Zero monitoring/alerting for throughput** — Quality Metrics §8 has "<leg>_failed rate", "double_charge ≥ 1", "p95 submit ACK > 5s". Missing: "queue_depth_per_tenant > plan_tier baseline", "renders/day_per_tenant > quota", "LTX_fallback_rate_agency_wide > threshold".

9. **Erasure cascade unoptimized for batch** — FR-VS-031 "zero-resíduo ≤24h". If agency has 100 renders in flight at delete-time, CASCADE DELETE is O(N) over renders/scenes/assets/nodes/edges. Zero indexing or batch-erasure SLA.

10. **No batch API or queue-polling contract** — Zero `POST /functions/v1/video-batch-render` endpoint. Zero `batch_id` state tracking. Operator must call `/video-faceless-render` 5× to submit 5 renders.

11. **No multi-tenant account hierarchy** — If agency operator runs sub-accounts (1 master tenant + N client sub-accounts), zero guidance on managing N provider credentials per sub-tenant and keeping quotas isolated.

---

### CONCRETE PROPOSED AMENDMENTS

**1. New FR-VS-033 (Batch Queueing Requirement)**
```
Agency operators can submit N render jobs in a single API call 
(batch envelope) with atomic pre-debit of N × VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER + 
proportional refund on partial failure. Batch jobs respect per-tenant 
concurrent-job quota (Starter:≤3, Pro:≤10, Enterprise:≤30); excess jobs 
queue with p95 queue-wait SLA. Quota exceeded → HTTP 429 (Too Many Requests).
Traces to PR-VS-020 (batch render — upgrade from Could Have to High/v1.0).
```

**2. New NFR-VS-018 (Scale-Batch SLA)**
```
Per-tenant concurrent-job capacity & queue-wait SLA:
  • Starter: ≤3 concurrent, p95 queue-wait ≤10 min
  • Pro: ≤10 concurrent, p95 queue-wait ≤5 min
  • Enterprise: ≤30 concurrent, p95 queue-wait ≤2 min

Renders queued >60 min auto-refund as SLA breach.
Measured via infra_health_logs (queue_depth by user_id + plan_tier) 
and video_renders (queued_at, running_at) deltas.
```

**3. New KPI-VS-019 (Agency Throughput Monitoring)**
```
Videos per tenant per day:
  SUM(state='done' AND created_at::date = current_date) 
  GROUP BY user_id, plan_tier

Target:
  • Starter: ≤3/day (enforce at submit; 402 quota_exhausted if exceeded)
  • Pro: ≤20/day
  • Enterprise: unrestricted

Dashboard widget in /dashboard/analytics/video-throughput.
Enforcement materialized in edge-fn (FR-VS-034 below).
```

**4. New KPI-VS-020 (LTX Capacity Planning)**
```
LTX i2v ZeroGPU allocation by plan tier:
  • Starter: ~0.5 GPU-min/day (≈1 render) in shared pool
  • Pro: 2 GPU-min/day (≈4 renders)
  • Enterprise: 5 GPU-min/day dedicated (or paid tier)

Motion leg falls back to Ken-Burns (0 GPU cost) if quota exhausted.
Fallback cost = 0 mco (budget-friendly).

KPI-VS-018 (LTX fallback rate) monitored per tier.
>60% fallback for Starter tier triggers nudge: "upgrade to Pro for full-motion renders".
```

**5. Data Model Amendments (06-data-model.md §2)**

*New table `batch_jobs`:*
```sql
CREATE TABLE batch_jobs (
  id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
  project_id uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  batch_size int NOT NULL CHECK (batch_size > 0),
  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
    CHECK (state IN ('pending','queued','running','done','partial_done','failed','partial_failed')),
  charged_mco_total int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (charged_mco_total >= 0),
  refunded_mco_total int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (refunded_mco_total >= 0),
  refund_reason text,
  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamptz DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);
```

*Extend `video_renders`:*
```sql
ALTER TABLE video_renders ADD COLUMN batch_id uuid REFERENCES batch_jobs(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
ALTER TABLE video_renders ADD COLUMN batch_position int;
ALTER TABLE video_renders ADD COLUMN priority int DEFAULT 5 CHECK (priority >= 0 AND priority <= 10);
ALTER TABLE video_renders ADD COLUMN queued_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE video_renders ADD COLUMN running_at timestamptz;

-- Indices for queue scheduling:
CREATE INDEX video_renders_user_state_idx ON video_renders (user_id, state);
CREATE INDEX video_renders_batch_position_idx ON video_renders (batch_id, batch_position);
CREATE INDEX video_renders_queued_idx ON video_renders (queued_at);
```

**6. New Process PROC-VS-009 (Batch Rendering Pipeline with Fair-Queueing)**
```
Orchestration of N-render batch submission:

1. [Batch Validation]
   - count ≤ plan_tier quota
   - total cost ≤ available mcoCoins
   - voice_consent if needed

2. [Atomic Pre-Debit]
   - deduct_mco_coins(user_id, N × VIDEO_FACELESS_RENDER, 'batch_renders')
   - 402 if saldo < cost (no rendering starts)

3. [Queue Enrollment]
   - INSERT batch_jobs(state='queued', batch_size=N)
   - INSERT video_renders × N (batch_id, state='queued_for_batch')

4. [Fair-Queueing Scheduling]
   - Per-tenant WFQ weight inversely proportional to current queue depth
   - Prevents starvation: tenant with 10 jobs waiting doesn't monopolize workers

5. [Worker Consumption]
   - Workers pull from queue (respecting WFQ priority)
   - UPDATE video_renders(running_at=now(), state='running') per render
   - Execute pipeline (Gemini→Pollinations→VoxCPM→Remotion→LTX→FFmpeg→QA→entrega)

6. [Aggregate Batch State]
   - UPDATE batch_jobs(state='done'/'partial_done'/'partial_failed'/'failed')
   - If partial failure: proportional refund via refund_mco idempotent by batch_id

Terminal states:
  • pending (pre-debit, not yet queued)
  • queued (waiting for worker capacity)
  • running (≥1 render in-progress)
  • done (all N renders succeeded)
  • partial_done (K/N succeeded, K < N)
  • partial_failed (K/N failed, K < N, but some succeeded)
  • failed (all N failed, entire batch refunded)
```

**7. New API Endpoint: POST /functions/v1/video-batch-render**
```typescript
interface BatchRenderRequest {
  batch_items: Array<{
    product_id?: string;
    topic?: string;
    scenes?: number;
    voice_consent?: boolean;
  }>;
  project_id?: string;
  delivery?: { telegram?: boolean };
}

interface BatchRenderResponse {
  batch_id: string;
  status: 'queued_for_batch';
  charged_mco_total: number;
  estimated_duration_min: number; // p95 E2E × (batch_size / concurrent_capacity)
  queue_position: number;
}

Error codes:
  401 — JWT invalid
  402 — saldo < total cost OR provider not configured
  409 — voice_consent required and not provided
  429 — batch_size > plan_tier quota (Too Many Requests; Retry-After: queue-wait estimate)
  422 — validation error (field + PT-BR message)
```

**8. New FR-VS-034 (Agency Quota Enforcement)**
```
Edge function video-faceless-render entrypoint checks tenant plan_tier quota:
  • Starter: ≤3 concurrent + ≤3/day
  • Pro: ≤10 concurrent + ≤20/day
  • Enterprise: unrestricted

Quota exceeded → HTTP 429 (Too Many Requests) with:
  - Retry-After header (ETA queue-wait in seconds)
  - Response body: { error: 'quota_exhausted', action: 'upgrade_or_wait', queue_position: 5 }

Quota state materialized in profiles:
  - render_concurrency_used (incremented on submit, decremented on terminal state)
  - render_daily_used (incremented on submit, reset daily 00:00 UTC per tenant timezone)

Zero silent queueing; quota breach is always API-observable.
```

**9. SDD §1 Post-GA Architecture (Horizontal Scale Path)**

Add subsection 1.5:
```
Scale Path (Post-GA):

MVP (single-replica n8n/remotion) supports ≤3 concurrent per NFR-VS-005.

Post-GA horizontal scaling (architecture-ready, not MVP-blocking):
  (A) n8n → k8s StatefulSet with N replicas, each attached to shared Redis Streams queue
  (B) Load-balancer routes edge-fn submissions to least-loaded n8n instance
  (C) remotion-render → separate k8s Deployment pool (independent autoscaling)
  (D) Per-tenant WFQ scheduler decouples tenant queue submit rate from shared worker pool capacity

R5 bridge unchanged (DNS loopback per tenant shared via multi-network attach).

Feasibility: k8s orchestration piloted post-MVP as 'scale-ready' design, not MVP-blocking.
Enables v1.0 agency TAM (50k agências rendering 10–100 videos/day).
```

**10. BRD §7 Constraint Amendment**

Add explicit constraint:
```
Per-tenant render quota enforcement is CRITICAL for fair-queueing at agency scale.

Without quota SLA (e.g., concurrent-render cap per plan tier), a single tenant 
can monopolize shared n8n/remotion-render indefinitely, stalling other agencies 
(starving-writes problem).

MVP gates on:
  • Single-render success rate ≥95%
  • p95 E2E render time ≤12 min

v1.0 gates on quota + WFQ SLA:
  • Quota enforcement provably tested (3+ concurrent tenants)
  • Queue depth monitored, no starvation >SLA per tier
  • LTX fallback telemetry per tenant (KPI-VS-020)
```

**11. PRD §3 TAM Expansion (Marketing Positioning)**

Expand Secondary segment description:
```
**Secondary (Agência-scale) TAM:** Micro-agências rendering 10–100 vídeos/day 
across client portfolios (primary use-case: monthly product catalog renders 
for 3–10 clients). Addressable segment ~50k agências BR (SOM ~6.8M R$/24m 
from existing model). 

Requires per-tenant throughput isolation, per-plan-tier quotas (Starter 3/day, 
Pro 20/day, Enterprise unlimited), and batch-submission APIs. 

MCORCH Video Studio v1.0 unlocks this via:
  • PROC-VS-009 batch queueing + fair-queueing scheduler
  • FR-VS-033/034 batch API + quota enforcement
  • NFR-VS-018/019 throughput SLAs + LTX capacity planning

Upsell hook: Agency reaching Starter 3/day quota →nudge to Pro (5× capacity, 
2-min queue-wait SLA vs 10 min, LTX 2 GPU-min/day vs 0.5). 

Estimated +R$ 50M/year TAM from tier upgrade (Starter→Pro→Enterprise) driven 
by volume growth over 24 months.
```

**12. Quality Metrics §4 Self-Healing Loop (New SOL-VS-009)**

```
Trigger: Queue depth per tenant exceeds plan_tier baseline for >10 min

Autonomous response (via mega-brain-daemon):
  (A) Attempt horizontal scale: add n8n replica if cluster headroom exists
  (B) Throttle new batch submissions from affected tenant: 402 queue_full, Retry-After
  (C) Escalate to Sovereign for capacity planning (incident alert)

Measured by:
  • infra_health_logs (queue_depth_by_user_id_plan_tier events)
  • KPI-VS-019 (renders/day per tenant vs quota)
  • KPI-VS-020 (LTX fallback rate >60% indicates capacity pressure)

Feedback: Aggregate anomalies → alert Sovereign; if >SLA persists 7 days → 
escalate to quarterly capacity planning meeting.
```

Scope: Add PROC-VS-009, FR-VS-033/034, NFR-VS-018/019, new KPIs, 
batch_jobs schema, /video-batch-render API, quota enforcement, 
WFQ scheduler design, post-GA scale path in SDD.

Acceptance Criteria:

  3. Queue depth telemetry in infra_health_logs (queue_depth event type)

  5. LTX fallback rate monitoring implemented (KPI-VS-020)

Gate to v1.0 release: AFTER MVP single-render stability proven (KPI-VS-001 ≥95%).
Estimated effort: 2–3 sprints (schema + API + scheduler + tests + docs).
Owner: Engineering + Sovereign (product strategy review).
```

---

## SUMMARY

The BoK **perfectly captures the single-render pipeline** (MVP: geração faceless E2E + edição HyperFrames determinística, ambas gateadas por JWT, mcoCoins, QA VLM). However, **agency leverage (the secondary TAM multiplier) is deferred to Future scope** with zero quota model, zero batch queueing, zero fair-queueing, and zero throughput SLA.

**The LTX ZeroGPU 5 GPU-min/day limit is the hidden hard bottleneck:** an agency rendering 10 faceless videos/day hits the free ceiling by 10am and falls back to Ken-Burns motion (quality loss) with zero mitigation SOP.

**Concrete remediation:** Elevate PR-VS-020 (batch render) from Could Have (Future) to **High (v1.0, after MVP stability)**. Materialize PROC-VS-009 (fair-queueing orchestration), FR-VS-033/034 (batch API + quota enforcement), NFR-VS-018/019 (throughput SLAs), and KPI-VS-019/020 (agency monitoring). Schema: add `batch_jobs` table + extend `video_renders` with queue coordination columns. This positions v1.0 as the true TAM unlock: agencies at scale, not solo creators only.

## FINAL ASSESSMENT: SCALE-BATCH COVERAGE IN VIDEO-STUDIO BoK

Based on comprehensive read of all 8 documents (00-index through 08-quality-metrics), here is the structured assessment:

### **DIMENSION**
Scale & batch production capacity for agency leverage (MCORCH's B2B2C TAM multiplier — MANY videos/day across client portfolios)

### **COVERAGE RATING: PARTIAL**

**What IS covered:**
- Single-render pipeline well-specified (MVP geração faceless E2E + HyperFrames determinística)
- Single-job SLAs: submit ACK p95 <3s, E2E p95 ~5–12 min (FRD §3)
- Capacity ceiling: ≥3 concurrent jobs stable per single-replica n8n/remotion (NFR-VS-005)
- LTX ZeroGPU fallback to Ken-Burns documented (FR-VS-016)

**What IS NOT covered (material gaps for agency leverage):**
1. **Zero per-tenant quota model** — No concurrent-render or renders-per-day limits by plan tier (Starter/Pro/Enterprise)
2. **LTX 5 GPU-min/day free limit is undocumented as hard bottleneck** — Agency rendering 10 videos/day hits ceiling by 10am; no paid tier or per-tenant GPU quota strategy
3. **No batch/campaign queueing architecture** — No FIFO vs priority queue, no weighted-fair-queueing (WFQ), no Redis queue broker
4. **No atomic pre-debit for batch** — 5-render batch = 5 separate deduct_mco_coins RPC calls; zero atomic "pre-debit 5×cost, refund unprocessed N"
5. **Zero queue-wait SLA beyond 3 jobs** — No p95 submit-to-running target, no max-queue-age auto-refund, no 429 Too Many Requests handling
6. **No tenant isolation** — Shared n8n/remotion single-instance; tenant A can monopolize indefinitely, starving tenant B
7. **Zero agency batch journey** — PRD Marina persona is per-CLIENT, not per-DAY; no "30 videos for 3 clients by EOD" scenario
8. **No throughput monitoring** — Quality Metrics §8 missing queue_depth_per_tenant, renders/day_per_tenant>quota, LTX_fallback_rate alerts
9. **Erasure cascade unoptimized** — O(N) DELETE assumes single-render ≤24h, not batch-scale performance
10. **No batch API** — Zero `/functions/v1/video-batch-render` endpoint, zero batch_id state machine

### **PROPOSED CONCRETE AMENDMENTS**

1. **New FR-VS-033 (Batch Queueing):** POST /video-batch-render accepts N renders with atomic pre-debit + proportional refund; quota exceeded → 429
2. **New NFR-VS-018 (Scale-Batch SLA):** Starter ≤3 concurrent/p95 10min queue-wait, Pro ≤10/5min, Enterprise ≤30/2min; >60min refund
3. **New KPI-VS-019 (Throughput):** Videos/tenant/day by plan; Starter ≤3/day, Pro ≤20/day, Enterprise unlimited
4. **New KPI-VS-020 (LTX Quota):** GPU-min/day per plan; Starter 0.5, Pro 2, Enterprise 5; >60% fallback → nudge upgrade
5. **Data Model:** Add `batch_jobs` table + extend `video_renders` with batch_id, batch_position, priority, queued_at, running_at
6. **New PROC-VS-009 (Batch Pipeline):** Validate → atomic pre-debit → queue WFQ → workers consume → proportional refund
7. **SDD §1.5 Post-GA Scale:** k8s StatefulSet n8n + Redis Streams queue + per-tenant WFQ scheduler

### **KEY FINDING**

The BoK **perfectly captures single-render MVP but defers agency scale (the real TAM multiplier) to Future**. The **LTX 5 GPU-min/day limit is the hidden hard constraint** preventing an agency from producing 10+ faceless videos/day without quality loss (Ken-Burns fallback). 

**v1.0 enablement:** Materialize PR-VS-020 (batch render) + per-tenant quota + fair-queueing + throughput SLAs. This transforms video-studio from a **solo-creator tool** into a **multi-tenant agency production platform**.
